### 113 subject events

- **1915.10** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  摔伤之后两天，房东女人在门厅拦住Katharina Bauer，楼里的Anna Moser正提着菜篮站在门边。房东女人说楼梯间不许孩子嬉闹，铁扶手磕坏要赔。Katharina Bauer把裹着布条的Klara往身后一拉，回了句“灯都没有，谁看得见台阶”。房东女人把话记在房租本边上，说下回修灯的钱从房租里扣。
  _effects: mood -0.07000000000000002; flag +stairs_accident_noted_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍从面包房门口拐进巷子，Klara把布袋夹在腋下，学着前面人的样子，用胳膊肘顶住后面人的篮子。窗口的伙计舀面粉时，Klara踮脚看着秤，直到半袋面倒进袋口。挤出人群时鞋跟被踩掉，Klara单脚跳了两步，在泥里摸回鞋，索性赤脚走回家。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1918.12** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-lose` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  煤车刚过去，路心还留着几块碎煤。Klara的铁环撞上去，弹到水沟边。Mitzi Lechner先一步把铁环踩住，说要拿一颗石弹子来换。Klara蹲下去掏口袋，膝盖在石板棱上蹭掉一块皮，换回铁环时裤腿已经渗出血点；Klara没再争，只记住Mitzi Lechner踩铁环的那只脚。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.04; condition_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Mitzi Weiss家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Klara递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Mitzi Weiss端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Klara把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

  你数出四十赫勒搁在秤盘上，说剩下的一克朗用那张旧折叠桌抵。老板撇了撇嘴，让伙计跟你上楼搬桌子。桌子腿有点晃，但木料还结实。邻居在楼道里搭了把手，账本上这一页只写了一行，没写利息。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买煤付的部分现钱); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; state_add political-suspect state:world-vienna-1912:76; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; flag +funeral_society_left; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

### 140 subject events

- **1913.02** `statistical_infection`
  Josef在Bauer-Wohnung, Leopoldstadt病倒了，痢疾让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1913.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Josef的痢疾没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1913.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Josef的痢疾没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1913.08** `statistical_recovery`
  Josef熬过了这场痢疾，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1917.03** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-lose` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  铁环滚进沟沿，Josef扑过去抓，膝盖先着了地。Mitzi Lechner把铁环拎起来，在手里转了一圈，说不能白捡。Josef摸出一颗石弹子递过去，Mitzi Lechner这才松手。回家路上Josef一瘸一拐，裤膝上沾着石屑和血，经过Mitzi Lechner家门口时特意绕到街对面。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.04; condition_

- **1918.01** `statistical_infection`
  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Grete Stein在绳子那头喊。Josef踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Grete Stein数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Josef把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Ferdinand Winter把Josef拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Josef把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Josef蹲下把两包并排，按Ferdinand Winter说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-ferdinand-winter; skill_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Katharina Bauer把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Josef伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Katharina Bauer从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Josef手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Josef一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer_

  汤喝到一半，Katharina Bauer放下勺子点了头，但说得很清楚：先攒出一套锅碗、一只挂钟，再把话往下说。Luise Huber在桌下用膝盖碰了碰Josef，Katharina Bauer把最后一块煮牛肉推过来，饭桌上的空气总算松了；这一顿让对方家里管了饭，自家灶上省下两克朗的吃食，也吃出一个带条件的准信。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1930.09** `statistical_infection`
  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1930.10** `statistical_recovery`
  Josef从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1931.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-by-the-day` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne只招日工。Josef天亮前到门口，名字写在临时名单上，干一天算一天，一天一克朗，月底结二十六克朗。工头说下个月可能有正式位置，也可能没有；Josef把每天的工时记在烟盒背面，怕漏掉一天。
  _effects: employment Tagelöhner @ org-winter-print; role; cash 2 (first daily pay advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; flag +day_labour_only; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

  _effects: cash -1 (书脊里的借条（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +lost_layaway_book; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; knowledge “the layaway book sold to a man in a leather coat”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1931.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton Lechner嫌Josef的床单滴水滴到Anton Lechner家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Josef下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Josef把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Anton Lechner那侧。

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Josef在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Josef把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Josef在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Josef的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.05 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; employment clerk @ org-die-fackel; state_transition university-student -> university-dropout; flag +university_dropout_

  Josef把报纸推到一边，和Anton Lechner聊起电车票价要涨的传闻。两人拿过糖罐当算盘，在桌布上比划通勤开销，两杯咖啡四十赫勒，争论得忘了时间，直到窗外的煤气灯亮起来。Josef起身时发现大衣下摆沾了咖啡渍，晚饭的点早过了，却约好下回还坐这张桌子。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (两杯咖啡的价钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; mood 0.040000000000000036_

  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: skill; relation friend with person-josefine-hofer; cash -1.2 (挂号寄稿的纸张与邮资); mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

  Josef替Katharina Lechner 2把名字、出生年和最后住址写在纸上，递进窗口。Luise Kral翻了三本名册，手指停在一行被水洇开的字上，说这个人登记在临时收容所。Katharina Lechner 2听见时，手里的伞掉在地上；Josef替她把伞捡起来，才发现自己的手也在抖。
  _effects: cash -2 (念错的名字（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +found_missing_relative_on_list; role; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; knowledge “the missing relative was registered at a transit shelter”_

- **1937.08** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.dock-morning/dock-picked-coal` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, dock
  同来的Grete Stein没被点上，骂了一声把麻绳甩上肩走了。Josef跟着工头进了货栈，煤块从跳板滑进船舱，到午后点了四克朗二十赫勒，手指黑得洗不净。
  _effects: cash 4.2 (卸煤半日的工钱)_

- **1937.08** `r3-mig.border-village::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/crossed-under-the-hay` tags: migration, border, village, smuggling, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  Maria Huber 2把Josef藏在干草下面，赶着马车走田埂。经过界碑时，Luise Kral抬手拦了一下，Maria Huber 2说车上是喂牛的草，Luise Kral用刺刀往草里扎了两下，没扎到底。马车走出半里地，Maria Huber 2才让Josef出来换气。
  _effects: cash -6 (教堂钟声两边都听得见（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +crossed_under_hay; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; knowledge “the patrol probes hay with a bayonet but not to the bottom”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

  Josef在夜里收拾了一只箱子，把证件缝进外套内衬，搭上往西的火车。Ferdinand Huber第二天来查人，只看到空床和没关严的窗。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -20 (escape and lodging（其中20克朗记入债务）); condition_

  他挑了朝院子的那间单间，付了一个月押金和半个月房租，房东点清钱才给钥匙。看门人把备用钥匙挂进门房的木板上，嘱咐他晚上十点以后别在楼梯上跺脚。屋里有张床、一只铁炉和半截蜡烛。
  _effects: cash -9 (单间一个月押金与半个月房租（其中9克朗记入债务）); residence; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

- **1938.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜把钥匙拴在门后的钉子上，又反过来压了压窗栓。车票是硬纸的，打孔时咔嗒一声。Josef在车厢里把诊断书摊在膝盖上，窗外最后几排厂房退远了，松树的味道从窗缝钻进来。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金（其中12克朗记入债务）); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1938.03** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.closed` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Josef把窗关上，争吵声立刻变成闷响，像隔着一层棉被。可那男人的一句话还是从缝里钻进来，她躺到床上翻来覆去，数着墙上的水渍，到很晚才睡着。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: cash -3 (偿还市场赊账（其中3克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

  Josef把最后一袋黑麦埋进煤堆下面，夜里听见巡逻队从窗前走过。第二天面包房照常开门，但货架上少了三排面包；Josef知道那不是因为城里没有粮。
  _effects: food; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +hidden_grain; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-emil-weiss_

  _effects: mood -0.16000000000000003; flag +friend_evacuated_before_farewell; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; knowledge “the friend left with an evacuation train”_

  Josef把一整袋面粉倒进Ferdinand Moser 2的空口袋，袋口用麻绳扎了两道，说等她男人发了薪再还。Ferdinand Moser 2连声道谢，把写着借煤日期的小纸片折好放回围裙。柜子里只剩一袋面粉，Josef当晚煮汤时多加了一勺水。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

  Josef走到半路风忽然大起来，围巾抽在脸上生疼，她掉头往回走。路过面包店时橱窗里还剩几个昨天的圆面包，四赫勒一个，她买了两个，揣在怀里带回去当晚饭。
  _effects: food; cash -0.08 (两个剩面包四赫勒一个（其中0.08克朗记入债务）)_

  星期天的点心拆开纸包，Mitzi Lechner先问他这半年干了什么。听完没多说，只说明天一早跟他走。料场边那个工头话不多，让他搬了两趟木箱，点了点头。

- **1940.05** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef没有立刻答话，先去敲Ferdinand Huber的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Ferdinand Huber提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Josef把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。

  负责人说这是不信任，Josef回说数目对得上才叫信任。Emil Weiss把卷角的账页压平，指着一行重复的支出，屋里安静得能听见煤气灯嘶声。最后决定下次集会再报账。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; reputation 0.04000000000000001; skill_

  _effects: food; flag +received_aid_society_help; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104; organization_membership; knowledge “the society serves soup until the pot is empty”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104_

  _effects: cash -1 (雨里的护照队（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +missed_passport_window; knowledge “passport numbers are exhausted before noon”_

  _effects: reputation -0.08000000000000002; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001042:child; cash -1 (互助会停止发放的补贴（其中1克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +reputation_damaged_by_betrayal_

- **1933.09** `r1-cul.faith-crisis::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/confessed-doubt` tags: faith, doubt, confession, church, grief, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa在告解席前跪了很久，最后只说了一句：我不知道还有没有人在听。Friedrich Hofer没有立刻回答，隔着帘子把一枚旧十字架放在窗台上。Emil Weiss在教堂门口等Rosa，看见Rosa出来时没有划十字，也没有问。
  _effects: condition; flag +confessed_doubt; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; knowledge “the priest left a worn cross on the window ledge”_

- **1933.10** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa把通知念给Luise Winter听，Luise Winter摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Rosa去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food_

- **1934.01** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, partnership
  Rosa和Mitzi Winter在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Mitzi Winter把煤钱推过来，Rosa接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1934.01** `r1-cul.fashion-purchase::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/ruined-by-rain` tags: culture, fashion, shop, money, vanity, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa在橱窗前站了三个下午，最后数出十二克朗，把帽子捧回家。第一次戴出去就下雨，帽檐塌下来，像一只湿透的鸟；Mitzi Winter把自己的旧毡帽换给Rosa，说这顶经得起雨。Rosa把新帽子晾在炉边，第二天缩了一圈，再也戴不上，十二克朗只剩下一圈硬边。
  _effects: cash -12 (橱窗里的帽子); condition; flag +hat_ruined_by_rain; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1934.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.cut-the-guarantee/settle-at-the-tavern` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  没有签字，陪对方去酒馆把账当面谈清。掌柜的把欠条一张张摆在桌上，一共二十八克朗。有人替着说了几句好话，讲明利息怎么算、按月还多少，请掌柜的宽限到月底。谈下来先还五克朗，余下的立了新据，担保人一栏空着，掌柜的临走时瞪了一眼。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (酒馆里替对方先垫的第一笔款子和两杯酒); relation friend with person-marie-huber; reputation -0.01999999999999999; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1934.09** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Rosa对Emil Winter说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Emil Winter把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Rosa的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

- **1934.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Friedrich Hofer嫌Rosa的床单滴水滴到Friedrich Hofer家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Rosa下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Rosa把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Friedrich Hofer那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-friedrich-hofer; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_

- **1935.01** `r13-work.advertise::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/painted-over` tags: commerce, advertising, shop, competition, literacy, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa一早去贴海报，发现墙上已经被Luise Kral 2的招贴盖住了，糨糊还是湿的。Rosa把湿纸揭下来，底下露出自己那张的一角。两人在墙边站了一会儿，Luise Kral 2说这条街只容得下一家做广告的；Rosa把剩下的海报抱回去，改贴在自家橱窗里。
  _effects: cash -1 (橱窗里的手写海报); flag +work13_advertising_poster_covered; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92_

- **1935.02** `r3-mig.exile-return::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/return-visa-granted` tags: migration, exile, return, politics, papers, vienna, urban, crisis
  Helene Stein把审查表翻到第三页，问Rosa在国外参加过什么组织。Rosa说只去过一次救济会。Helene Stein在“无政治活动”一栏打了勾，给了一张回国许可，说“先去警察局报到”。Katharina Bauer在门口等着，手里提着一瓶酒。
  _effects: mood 0.07999999999999996; condition; flag +return_visa_granted; relation friend with person-katharina-bauer; knowledge “returning exiles must report to the police”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1935.03** `r2-law.loan-shark::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/signed-weekly-interest` tags: finance, debt, usury, poverty, threat, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa在Anna Stein 2的账本上按了手印，借十克朗，周息五分。Anna Stein 2把钱数在柜台上，又抽回一枚，说那是“跑腿费”。Elisabeth Moser在门口看着，等Anna Stein 2进屋才低声说，这条街上没人还得清他的第二笔。
  _effects: cash 9 (五分利，按周算); condition; flag +took_shark_loan; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; knowledge “the lender charges five percent weekly plus a collection fee”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

- **1935.10** `r2-law.residency-permit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/permit-renewed` tags: law, residency, papers, police, housing, vienna, urban, crisis
  Luise Kral把居留证翻到住址页，说章盖的是旧地址，和申报的不一样。Rosa解释上个月搬了家，Ferdinand Huber在门外探头，说合同是从月初算的。Luise Kral让Ferdinand Huber写一张确认书，又让Rosa按手印；章盖下去时，正好压住了旧地址的半行字。
  _effects: cash -5 (居留章盖在旧地址上); flag +residency_permit_renewed; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; knowledge “a landlord’s confirmation can correct the address on a resid”_

- **1935.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Josefine Hofer一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Josefine Hofer的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-josefine-hofer; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1936.01** `r1-work.porter-luggage::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/found-the-missing-case` tags: work, porter, station, luggage, accusation, vienna, urban, crisis
  Katharina Bauer说箱子是棕色的，Rosa在行李房里一排排看号牌，最后在最底层找到一只被压歪的箱子。锁扣开了，里面的银餐具还在。Katharina Bauer把两克朗塞进Rosa手里，Ferdinand Huber在门口记下时间，笔尖在纸上停了一停。
  _effects: cash 2 (行李房里的十七号牌); flag +found_missing_luggage; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

- **1936.01** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.quarrel` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  铁桶磕在石沿上，水晃出来打湿了Rosa的裙角。Josefine Hofer的声音拔高，说轮到她就是轮到她。Rosa咬着嘴没再喊，把盆往自己门前拖，湿石板上一道水痕。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

- **1936.01** `state_expire:failed-apprentice` tags: failed-apprentice, craft, work, failure, stigma, state, state_tick, state:failed-apprentice
  Rosa的「学徒契约终止」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove failed-apprentice state:world-vienna-1912:57_

- **1936.01** `p10-family-tasks.arrange-childcare/arrange-childcare.quit-job` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, childcare
  厂里说半天工没了就没了，Rosa没再争。厨房里少了一只面包，晚饭只有一锅糊糊。伴侣把勺子放下，说来年春天再说。夜里Rosa替邻居补袜子，三个赫勒一双，缝到灯芯烧短。
  _effects: cash -4 (辞掉半天工少了一份收入); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

- **1936.02** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/shifts-with-sibling` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Rosa和Mitzi Lechner把值班时辰写在门后的粉笔板上：Mitzi Lechner守前半夜，Rosa守后半夜，医生说静养二十一天。煤油灯芯拧小，药粉分成小包摆在床头，便盆换水时尽量不碰出响。二十一天熬下来，Rosa的眼皮肿了，走路发飘，但Rosa Lechner每次起身总有人扶。
  _effects: relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner; flag +night_care_duty_

- **1936.06** `r3-war.desertion-mp::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hid-deserter` tags: war, desertion, military_police, shelter, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  Alois Winter敲谷仓门时，靴子上全是泥，手里攥着半张撕掉的地图。Rosa让他进来，用干草盖住脚印；Hermann Weiss 3在楼上咳嗽了一声，没下楼。天亮前，Grete Stein的摩托车在院外熄了火，Rosa把Alois Winter推进地窖，自己坐在门槛上削土豆。
  _effects: flag +hid_deserter_from_military_police; role; relation rival with person-grete-stein; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

- **1937.01** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/burn-the-letter` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  等到第九天，Rosa把没寄出的草稿和那封写了一半的信一起塞进炉子。纸卷起来，火苗发蓝，屋里一股焦味；Rosa拿火钩把灰捅散，决定不再去问信的下落。炉门上的铁片烫手，那个“亲”字先没了。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +gave_up_on_letter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

- **1937.01** `p9-work-career.clerk-post/p9-work-career.clerk-post.archive` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, office, application
  Rosa的信写得好，笔画干净，算盘也快，可速记一栏空了大半。Grete Stein把卷子批了“文书可用，速记欠熟”，人事科把他安排到档案室抄旧保单，日薪两克朗四十赫勒，比正式办事员少一截。桌旁没有窗，只有一盏悬在头顶的灯，他抄到下班时右肩发僵。
  _effects: cash 2.4 (档案室抄写日薪); skill; flag +awaiting_clerk_slot_

- **1937.03** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.advance-from-fund` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  互助金的铁盒上了两把锁，钥匙一把在Rosa手里，一把在邻街的面包师那里。取钱时面包师说“月底前要补回来”。药买回来了，雇主那边的人说手续“走流程”，说完就去忙别的。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); health 0.05999999999999999 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000154:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1937.03** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.buy-coal` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  炉门一开，黑烟先扑出来，Rosa眯着眼把纸团往里塞。火苗舔着碎煤慢慢亮起来，Grete Berg隔着门喊当心一氧化碳。水壶开始咕噜作响，屋里才有点热乎气。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (购买一小筐碎煤); food_

- **1937.09** `r1-fam.friendship-formation::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/friendship.bucket` tags: social, friendship, neighbor, water, trust, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa和Anna Stein因为一只水桶吵了一架，谁也不肯先开口。过了半个月，Anna Stein家的孩子病了，Rosa把自家的退烧药分了一半放在她门口，没有留名字。第二天，水桶被放回了原处，桶底多了一块洗得发白的布。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +reconciled_over_a_water_bucket; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/resists` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Rosa在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Anton Huber 3记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Rosa带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work)_

- **1938.01** `hist15-occ.requisition-notice::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/name-the-neighbor` tags: occupation, requisition, shortage, institution, history, family, vienna, urban
  Rosa告诉Luise Kral，Anton Lechner家的地窖里还有两桶猪油。征用队下午就去了那户人家。Rosa拿到一小袋盐和一张优先配给卡，回家时没有走平时那条街。
  _effects: cash 1 (检举邻居得到的配给奖励); reputation 0.01999999999999999; flag +denounced_neighbor_for_grain_

- **1938.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.got-ticket` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Rosa挤到窗口前，把一克朗硬币推进凹槽，换回一张印着座号的薄纸片。顶层站席的栏杆被前面的人汗湿，Rosa侧身找了个能看见舞台边的位置，倚着烫手的铁栏看完两幕；散场时小腿发麻，嘴里还哼着刚听到的曲调，幕间也和邻座搭上了两句话。
  _effects: cash -1 (约瑟夫施塔特剧院顶层站票); mood 0.05999999999999994; skill_

- **1938.08** `kernel-health.street-accident::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/accident-concussion` tags: health, accident, tram, insurance, street, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa在结冰的路面上滑倒，后脑磕到石阶。醒来时Anton Moser蹲在旁边，手里攥着Rosa的工票；医院观察了一夜，说是脑震荡，第二天让回家，账单却拖了三个月。工票上的油墨被汗水晕开，名字缺了一角。
  _effects: condition; role; cash -10 (hospital observation and a delayed bill); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; knowledge “脑震荡只观察一夜就让回家，账单可以拖三个月；工票在同事手里比在口袋里安全。”_

  Rosa没有回家，连夜走了三条街，把睡下的邻居一个个叫起来。有人穿着睡衣签，有人让孩子代签，还有人问签了会不会丢工。天亮时，纸上的名字凑到五百零四，最后一行是一个不识字的老太太按的手印。
  _effects: cash -1 (第五百个签名); reputation 0.07999999999999999; flag +collected_five_hundred_signatures; role; skill; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000429:child; relation friend with person-katharina-bauer_

  _effects: cash -2 (通行证手续费); role; flag +work_permit_granted; knowledge “城西修配厂只认盖蓝章的通行证。”_

  怀表是母亲陪嫁，表盖上有道划痕。Rosa用指甲把表盖擦亮，说这个就够。兄弟把欠杂货店的账认下来，十一克朗。两人下楼时兄弟走在前面，到门口停了一下，说下个月一起去。
  _effects: relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: food; flag +shared_bread_at_oven; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001042:child; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

  Rosa把名单重新抄了一遍，把真名换成住址，把住址换成母亲的名字。Gustav Lechner 2问这样还能找到人吗，Rosa说能，只要我们还活着。抄到最后一个名字时，楼下的警棍又敲了三下水管。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; condition; flag +recopied_society_list; role; skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; knowledge “the list now uses mothers names instead of members names”_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; reputation 0.04000000000000001; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

  _effects: health -0.04999999999999999 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

  Anna Stein把招租纸从门上揭下来，领Rosa看了后间。屋里只有一张铁床和一只裂了口的洗手盆，窗户对着天井。Rosa付了六克朗，Anna Stein把钥匙从围裙里解下来，说晚上十点关大门。
  _effects: cash -6 (新城区门牌下的招租纸); flag +lodging_secured_in_new_district; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; knowledge “the front door is locked at ten every night”_

- **1947.03** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Rosa只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Ferdinand Moser 2手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Ferdinand Moser 2把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Rosa记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

  Rosa没有争，把遗嘱交给Anton Huber 3存档，自己只拿了父亲的一只烟斗。Mitzi Lechner后来把房子卖了，钱分了一半送来。Alois Moser 3说这院子以后没梨吃了；Rosa把烟斗点上，说树还在别人家院子里。
  _effects: cash 8 (遗嘱第三页被水泡过); flag +settled_inheritance_without_dispute; relation colleague with person-mitzi-lechner; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; knowledge “the house was sold and the proceeds split in half”_

- **1947.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money

  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用（其中1克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1948.01** `r13-edu.night-class::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory::factory-canteen-class/overtime-whistle` tags: education, night_class, adult_education, adult_evening_school, work, numeracy, vienna, urban
  Rosa刚坐下，汽笛就响了，夜班提前半小时开工。Rosa把没写完的作业塞进工具箱，第二天发现纸被机油浸透，只剩几个数字还认得出来。那批活赶完，Rosa领到加班费，却把夜校的课本忘在工具箱里整整一周。油墨把页码糊住了。
  _effects: cash 2 (下班铃后的夜校); mood -0.06000000000000005; condition_

  柜台上摊着两张工资袋，一张油墨新，一张已经磨毛了边。Rosa把两袋并排放，掌柜伸出食指把旧的拨到一边，说“你管好自己的”。师兄在窗口看街对面修鞋摊，一直没回头。
  _effects: reputation -0.04; skill_

  琴箱的螺丝松了，每转一圈都发出吱呀声，可圆舞曲还是圆舞曲。她靠着墙听完，看见三楼窗台有人用绳子吊下一只小篮子收钱，绳子在风里晃。她摸出铜板时，猴子已经蹲在她脚边了。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (给手摇风琴师的赏钱（其中0.02克朗记入债务）); mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Helene 2。接生的人收六克朗，Ferdinand Moser 2在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Rosa起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中4克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:312_

  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +trained_apprentice; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004651:child_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; food; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

  _effects: cash -3 (从殖民地回来的皮箱（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +took_in_returning_colonist; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; knowledge “the returnee has land deeds with no stamps”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-130_

  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

  Rosa把结婚戒指从手上褪下来，指节上留了一圈白印。当铺给了十二克朗，Grete Berg 4收六克朗，剩下的刚够买一只鸡和半袋面。Ferdinand Moser 2晚上回来，看见Rosa的手，什么也没问，只把自己的戒指也摘下来，放进同一个抽屉。
  _effects: cash 6 (接生婆的收据); condition; flag +sold_ring_for_midwife; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; birth male; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:336_

  _effects: cash -8 (汽车喇叭惊了马（其中7克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +hit_and_run_witness; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; knowledge “the car belongs to someone who parks behind the city hall”_

  Rosa难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Helene 2；Rosa一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Ferdinand Moser 2把钟表送进当铺。Rosa落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; cash -20 (midwife and hospital（其中20克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:343_

  Rosa说自己也等钱交房租，把口袋翻给Franz Lechner 4看。Franz Lechner 4没有再求，转身去敲另一扇门；第二天，楼道里有人说Rosa见死不救，Rosa听见了，只把煤筐往门里挪了挪。那天夜里，两家都早早关了灯。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-133; role; reputation -0.03; flag +loan_refused; knowledge “邻居转身去敲了别人的门，楼道里开始议论这件事。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-133_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Rosa和门房为号码顺序争起来，被推到街边，帽子滚进水沟。傍晚回来时窗口已经关了，长椅上空空荡荡，只剩一张踩脏的挂号单；Rosa在湿冷街头耗了一天，咳嗽更深，诊所门口的人也都记住了这张吵红的脸。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; reputation -0.019999999999999997_

  Rosa拿不出押金，就在市场租了一个半张桌子的摊位，卖线和纽扣。第一天只卖出三卷线，Anton Huber 3的摊子却排着队。Rosa收摊时把剩货装进木箱，决定明天早点来，占住靠门的那一头。
  _effects: cash -6 (租下街角的空铺（其中6克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +started_as_stallholder; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-155_

  Luise Winter说丈夫是“在睡梦中走的”，但邻居告诉Rosa，死者是在酒馆后巷冻死的。Rosa把两种说法都写进稿子，Maria Huber删掉了后一句。报纸出来时，Luise Winter在报社门口站了很久，手里攥着那份讣告。

  Otto 2用占领军发的纸马克买Rosa的鸡蛋，纸币上印着一只鹰，摸起来像旧报纸。Rosa按黑市价算，Otto 2又加了一包烟；Berta Moser 3在门口看着，说“别让Otto Lechner 2看见”。交易完，Rosa把纸马克压在米缸底下。

  Rosa把银餐具裹进围裙，塞给Elisabeth Winter，说“先放你家煤窖”。Luise Kral来查时，只看见空木盒和一张旧照片。Elisabeth Winter晚上把围裙送回来，说“煤窖潮，银器会黑”。Rosa把围裙藏在床垫下面，一夜没睡。

  “你看看这角。”Mitzi Weiss把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Emil伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Mitzi Weiss从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Emil手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Emil一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Ferdinand Huber把Emil拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Emil把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Emil蹲下把两包并排，按Ferdinand Huber说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1920.09** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-win` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷子窄，两个孩子并排推着铁环，铁环撞在一起叮当作响。Emil的钩子先绕过最后一块界石。Josef不服，说再比一次，手却已经伸进口袋，摸出三颗彩色弹珠。Emil把弹珠举到眼前看，Josef在旁边踢墙，踢得鞋尖发白。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.05; skill_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Emil拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Josefine Hofer弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Emil接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Emil前面的大婶一直往后靠，Emil就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Emil的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill_

  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

  _effects: relation partner with person-luise-kral; cash -0.04 (两小袋烤栗子); mood 0.06_

- **1936.01** `r13-civ.rehearse-play::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/rehearsal-broken-up` tags: culture, theatre, rehearsal, play, ambition, vienna, urban, crisis

  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +rehearsal_broken_up_by_landlord; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1936.04** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena把通知念给Gustav Berg听，Gustav Berg摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Lena去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中2克朗记入债务）); food; mood -0.03_

- **1936.06** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/nod-and-pots` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  汤喝到一半，Josefine Hofer放下勺子点了头，但说得很清楚：先攒出一套锅碗、一只挂钟，再把话往下说。Mitzi Bauer 2在桌下用膝盖碰了碰Lena，Josefine Hofer把最后一块煮牛肉推过来，饭桌上的空气总算松了；这一顿让对方家里管了饭，自家灶上省下两克朗的吃食，也吃出一个带条件的准信。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

- **1936.11** `r3-mig.residency-registration::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/landlady-refused-signature` tags: migration, registration, housing, papers, bureaucracy, vienna, urban, crisis
  Ferdinand Huber把登记表推过来，要房东签字证明Lena住在那里。Mitzi Weiss在窗口外听见，立刻说自己只是合租，不能替人担责。Ferdinand Huber把表收回，在户籍卡上盖了“地址不明”。Lena当晚回去，门锁已经换了，房东隔着门说登记的事找别处。
  _effects: mood -0.1; condition; flag +registration_refused_by_landlady; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; knowledge “a landlord’s signature is required for registration”_

- **1937.01** `p9-history-union-political.reform-meeting/reform-hand-to-union` tags: politics, union, party, meeting, leaflet, police, strike, reform
  Lena把请愿书递给工会代表，自己退到后排靠柱子的位置。Mitzi Berg 3从旁边递来一支短铅笔，让Lena把台上说的数目记在请柬背面，回家再誊清楚。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1937.01** `r13-work.interview-prep::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/slept-through` tags: work, interview, shop, preparation, class, vienna, urban, crisis
  Lena把硬领放在椅背上，坐着背到后半夜，天亮时却睡过了头。跑到店门口，Grete Berg 2正在关账房的窗。Lena说路上电车坏了，Grete Berg 2看了一眼Lena没系好的领扣，说下个月再来问吧。
  _effects: cash -1 (借来的硬领); mood -0.09999999999999998_

- **1937.02** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.wash-day/wash-day.listen-gossip` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, laundry, neighbor, household
  肥皂沫从Katharina Lechner的指缝里冒出来，她说话时手里不停，衣领在搓板上蹭得吱吱响。Lena把湿被单搭上绳子，被单沉甸甸地坠下来，她踮脚才够到第二根竹竿。
  _effects: mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1937.08** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Lena把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Katharina Lechner 2放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Alois Kral在门口看了一眼，没多话。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

- **1937.08** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.rush` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  木盆边的水渍映着天光，Lena跪在石阶上揉搓床单，肘弯发酸。晾绳上的湿布滴答作响，Katharina Lechner 2探身把自家被单也挂上去，两人没多说话。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/resists` tags: politics, persecution, 1938, injury
  Lena在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Ferdinand Huber记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Lena带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: health -0.11999999999999998 (beaten in police custody); condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work)_

- **1938.01** `hist15-occ.work-permit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/permit-granted` tags: occupation, work, permit, institution, shortage, history, vienna, urban

  _effects: cash -2 (通行证手续费); role; flag +work_permit_granted; knowledge “城西修配厂只认盖蓝章的通行证。”_

  Lena把一页讣告的活字排完，数了行数，发现Luise Kral把空行也算进了钱。Grete Berg 2说这是老规矩，Lena却把空行一根根抽出来，重新码在字盘里。Luise Kral过来看了一眼，说以后按实际行数算。
  _effects: cash 2 (计件单上的小数点); flag +work13_counted_type_lines; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1938.03** `r2-law.pawn-ticket::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/watch-auctioned` tags: finance, pawnshop, debt, household, loss, vienna, urban, crisis
  过了期限，Luise Moser把银表摆进拍卖柜，起价是当初借款的一半。Lena站在人群后面，看见一个陌生人举了三次牌。Ferdinand Huber在门口登记买主姓名，问Lena是不是来赎当的；Lena说不是，只是来看看表走得准不准。
  _effects: cash -4 (过期的当票); mood -0.14; condition; flag +pawned_watch_sold_at_auction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; knowledge “the watch sold for half the original loan”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1938.05** `vienna-women-housework.care-old-parent/care.institution` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  医院走廊里一股石炭酸味，Lena把包袱交给护士，里面是两件换洗衣裳。Josefine Hofer拉着她的手说了几句，听不清。回来路过教堂，她没进去，只在台阶上坐了一会儿。
  _effects: cash -8 (支付母亲住院的押金与费用); health 0.019999999999999997 (夜间不再需要频繁起身，睡眠好转); mood -0.05999999999999994; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1939.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Lena在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Lena把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05000000000000002 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007046_

- **1939.01** `hist15-occ.requisition-notice::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/hide-the-last-sack` tags: occupation, requisition, shortage, institution, history, family, vienna, urban
  Lena把最后一袋黑麦埋进煤堆下面，夜里听见巡逻队从窗前走过。第二天面包房照常开门，但货架上少了三排面包；Lena知道那不是因为城里没有粮。
  _effects: food; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +hidden_grain_

- **1939.01** `r13-edu.numeracy-class::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/catches-short-wage` tags: education, numeracy, adult_education, wages, work, vienna, urban, crisis
  Lena在夜校学会算百分比，回家把工钱袋倒在桌上，一笔一笔对。工头少算了三个加班钟点，Lena第二天拿着纸去找Luise Kral，Luise Kral看了半天，补了两克朗，说以后别在车间里声张。那两克朗Lena没花，夹在账本里，旁边写着“加班三小时”。
  _effects: cash 4 (算盘上的工钱); flag +checked_wage_slip; skill_

  Gustav Lechner 2听说Lena以前在厂里和工头吵过架，把钥匙收回口袋，说铺子不租给“惹事的人”。Lena站在门口，看见玻璃上自己的影子，背后是Maria Huber刚擦干净的招牌。回家后，Lena把攒下的钱重新包进手帕。
  _effects: cash -2 (租下街角的空铺); mood -0.12; condition; flag +refused_shop_lease; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

  Lena把工钱倒在柜台上，一枚一枚数给Grete Berg 2听。Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)说上周迟到两次，扣的是“规矩”，不是工钱。Lena问规矩写在哪一张纸上，柜台后面没有人回答。
  _effects: cash -2 (少了两克朗的信封); relation rival with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the employer deducts wages without a written rule”_

  Lena把烧剩的货架清单交给Helene Stein，上面多了三箱根本没进过店的袜子。Helene Stein用铅笔在“库存”一栏画了个圈，问进货单在哪里。Mitzi Bauer 2从柜台底下抽出一叠纸，最上面那张的日期是火灾后第二天。
  _effects: cash 22 (烧焦的账本会说话); condition; flag +inflated_fire_loss_claim; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; knowledge “the inventory list includes goods that never entered the sho”_

  _effects: cash -4 (被征用的粮食折价); food; flag +requisition_receipt; knowledge “征用收据只在下一批检查前有效。”_

- **1940.01** `r2-leisure.concierge-medicine::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop::concierge-medicine-war/broke-the-medicine-cabinet` tags: hospitality, hotel, medicine, service, emergency, vienna, urban, crisis
  战时药品短缺，旅馆把客人的药锁在经理室的铁柜里，钥匙由Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung随身带着。半夜，Luise Moser心口疼，Lena去敲经理室的门，Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung说钥匙在军营，回不来。Lena砸开铁柜，玻璃划破了手。Luise Moser吃上药时，Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung在门口看着，没有报警。
  _effects: health -0.04999999999999999 (夜班柜台上的药瓶); reputation 0.05; condition; flag +broke_medicine_cabinet; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; knowledge “the manager keeps guest medicine locked in his office”_

- **1940.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.negotiate-hours/negotiate-hours.longer-break-only` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, hours, saturday
  Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung把柜台擦了两遍才开口，说午休可以到三刻钟，收铺的时辰照旧。Lena把算好的话咽回去，正午的钟一响，他多坐了会儿，看窗外的车过了一趟又一趟。
  _effects: health 0.020000000000000018 (午休稍长喘了口气); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation employer with person-sigmund-freud_

  Mitzi Winter告诉所有人Lena偷了东西。没有人找到赃物，但每个人都在传。Lena去市场时，摊主把零钱放在柜台上，不再递到手里。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; reputation -0.1; flag +false_accusation; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; state_add public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:190_

  _effects: food; health 0.020000000000000018 (喝上了热鱼汤)_

  _effects: cash -5 (工厂门口的传单); reputation -0.04; flag +fined_for_distributing_leaflets; knowledge “the fine must be paid at the district office within fourteen”_

  _effects: state_add institution-purge state:world-vienna-1912:206; mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +saw_the_purge_list; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

  Lena点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Katharina Lechner 2量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Lena把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Emil Weiss抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Lena把床单对折，腾出一半，Emil Weiss用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: position 代理科长 @org-wednesday-psychological-society; state_parameter institution-purge.vacancies 2 -> 1; role; knowledge “代理科长的前任只在这个位置上坐了四个月。”_

  _effects: state_remove public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:190_

- **1943.01** `r13-fam.care-for-elder::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::elder-care-war/elder-left-behind` tags: task, care, elder, household, health, intergenerational, vienna, urban
  伤兵站只肯留能走动的老人，Emil Weiss被挪到走廊尽头的临时铺位。Lena每天去送饭，看见老人的床挨着痰盂，枕头下压着自家的门牌。Lena把门牌收进衣袋，说明年开春就接你回去；老人点点头，没有问明年是哪一年。
  _effects: cash -2 (给床上的老人翻个身); mood -0.16000000000000003; condition; flag +elder_left_in_corridor; relation friend with person-emil-weiss; knowledge “the elder keeps the old door plate under the pillow”_

- **1943.03** `r3-war.liberation-flag::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/raised-flag-on-liberation-day` tags: war, liberation, occupation, flag, aftermath, vienna, urban, crisis
  第一面旗挂反了，蓝边朝上，Maria Huber在梯子上骂了一句，Lena在下面扶着梯子笑。街角还有零星的枪声，Josef Berg把藏了三年的葡萄酒搬出来，瓶塞一开，泡沫喷了Lena一脸。Luise Kral带着人从教堂那边过来，说“先别庆祝，仓库还在烧”。
  _effects: mood 0.16000000000000003; flag +raised_flag_on_liberation_day; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001390:child; knowledge “the depot is still burning”_

- **1943.05** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Katharina Winter排在前面，回头替Lena看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Lena用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000573:child; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1943.09** `r1-cul.faith-crisis::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/confessed-doubt` tags: faith, doubt, confession, church, grief, vienna, urban, crisis
  Lena在告解席前跪了很久，最后只说了一句：我不知道还有没有人在听。Ferdinand Huber没有立刻回答，隔着帘子把一枚旧十字架放在窗台上。Franz Moser在教堂门口等Lena，看见Lena出来时没有划十字，也没有问。
  _effects: condition; flag +confessed_doubt; knowledge “the priest left a worn cross on the window ledge”_

- **1944.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/difficult-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Lena难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Katharina Bauer；Lena一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Josef把钟表送进当铺。Lena落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; condition; cash -20 (midwife and hospital（其中20克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:239_

- **1944.01** `kernel-work.task-household-debt::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/work-debt-new-loan` tags: task, work, debt, household, credit, vienna, urban, crisis
  Lena还不上旧账，只好向Helene Stein介绍的放贷人再借十克朗，月息两成。Rosa收了旧账，又重新开了一页赊账；Lena在两本账上都按了手印。回家路上，Lena把两张借据分开藏，怕家里人看见。
  _effects: cash 10 (借新债还旧账); flag +new_debt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “放贷人的月息是两成，借据要分开藏。”; condition_

- **1944.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Katharina Bauer这周主动擦了黑板。Lena把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010720_

- **1944.04** `r2-law.building-permit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/structural-crack` tags: law, building_permit, construction, housing, inspection, vienna, urban, crisis
  验脚手架那天，Alois Winter用锤子敲了敲新砌的墙，声音发空。Helene Stein让拆一块砖看看，里面是碎砖填的，没有砂浆。Alois Winter说材料不够，先填上再说；Helene Stein把批文收回去，说这房子住不得人。Lena站在院子里，看见二楼的窗框已经歪了半指。
  _effects: condition; flag +building_found_structurally_unsafe; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000447:child; knowledge “the new wall is filled with rubble instead of mortar”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103_

- **1944.06** `r1-civ.suffrage-tea-room::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/amended-suffrage-petition` tags: politics, suffrage, petition, women, meeting, vienna, urban, crisis
  Lena站起来说，请愿书不该只写寡妇和女工，也该写女佣和洗衣妇。Grete Stein说这样会被驳回，Katharina Lechner 2却让Lena把话写在背面。散会时，签名簿多了一页，字迹挤在一起，像一群不肯排队的人。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; flag +amended_suffrage_petition; role; skill; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; knowledge “the amendment adds domestic servants and laundresses to the ”_

- **1944.08** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.bridge` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river
  Lena在桥边站了一会儿，看一艘平底船靠岸卸煤，几个工人用铁锹往岸上甩。风把煤灰吹到她脸上，她抹了一把，往回走时脚步比来时轻，回到巷口还给自己买了块三赫勒的糖。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; cash -0.03 (买了三赫勒的糖（其中0.03克朗记入债务）)_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Lena躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Lena去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1945.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/difficult-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child

  Anton Moser把Lena藏在干草下面，赶着马车走田埂。经过界碑时，Anton Huber 3抬手拦了一下，Anton Moser说车上是喂牛的草，Anton Huber 3用刺刀往草里扎了两下，没扎到底。马车走出半里地，Anton Moser才让Lena出来换气。
  _effects: cash -6 (教堂钟声两边都听得见（其中6克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +crossed_under_hay; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; knowledge “the patrol probes hay with a bayonet but not to the bottom”_

  Lena把Katharina Bauer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Katharina Bauer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Lena听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 31; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  铁门只开了一条缝，Luise Kral 2把人数到一百二十，才放最前面十个进去。Lena把冻裂的手摊在灯下，指节上的茧替她答了话。Luise Kral 2在纸角写了个“明早六点”，Elisabeth Berg 2被推到一边，脚边的麻袋口还开着，土豆滚进了排水沟。
  _effects: cash 2 (铁门开了一条缝); flag +work13_hired_at_factory_gate; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; employment Tagelöhner @ org-winter-print; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70_

- **1945.06** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.keep` tags: home, market, food, social
  扁豆汤的香味从门缝飘出去，Elisabeth Moser敲了门。Lena掀开锅盖又盖上，说只够自家吃。Elisabeth Moser什么也没说就走了。夜里Lena听见隔壁有人用勺子刮碗底。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1946.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/dismissed-after-accident` tags: adulthood, labour, injury, unemployment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故后第三天通知Lena不用再来了，说岗位不能一直空着。Lena拿着医院证明去工会，诊断书被工头压在抽屉里；治伤花去六克朗，Lena的手还没好，工作先没了，工牌交回去时手指还缠着纱布。
  _effects: unemployment (dismissed after accident); cash -6 (medical treatment（其中6克朗记入债务）)_

- **1946.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/stillbirth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle
  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Josef在楼梯间坐了很久；Lena在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Lena的名字；此后邻居提起Lena，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。
  _effects: cash -6 (midwife and burial（其中6克朗记入债务）); role_

  Luise Kral把征用单按在柜台上，Lena家里的两袋面粉被划走一袋，换回一张盖蓝章的收据。Luise Kral说下周还会来，如果地窖里再翻出粮食，收据就不作数了。
  _effects: cash -4 (被征用的粮食折价（其中4克朗记入债务）); food; knowledge “征用收据只在下一批检查前有效。”_

  Anton Huber 3在门框上画了一道白线，说十天内不许出门，菜和煤会有人送到楼梯口。Lena把线内的鞋一双双收进柜子，数了数米缸，又数了数窗台上的药包。第八天，送来的面包已经发硬。
  _effects: cash -2 (门框上的石灰线（其中0.2克朗记入债务）); food; mood -0.040000000000000036; condition; flag +observed_quarantine; knowledge “the quarantine lasts ten days and food is left at the stairh”_

- **1947.03** `r3-crime.false-accusation::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-housing/maid-confesses` tags: crime, court, servant, class, injustice, vienna, urban, postwar
  就在法官要敲槌时，Elisabeth Kral 2忽然站起来，说糖罐是她打碎的，碎片埋在院子里的李树下。Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)的脸涨得通红，Otto Lechner 2被叫去后院挖了半个钟头。Lena被当庭释放，门外下着雨，Elisabeth Kral 2追出来，把一个纸包塞进Lena手里，里面是三个月的工钱。
  _effects: reputation 0.05000000000000002; flag +cleared_by_maid_confession; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109; knowledge “the sugar bowl was broken and buried in the yard”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-130_

  Lena没有开门，只隔着门板说家里有孩子发烧。Alois Kral在门外站了一会儿，把一张写着地址的纸条从门缝塞进来。第二天，Lena照着地址送了一包面包，屋子里已经空了，桌上留着一只没洗的杯子。
  _effects: cash -1 (半夜的第三下敲门); mood -0.1100000000000001; flag +turned_away_fugitive_but_sent_food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; knowledge “the fugitive left an address under the door”_

  医院派来一名实习医生，身后跟着两个学生。他们把Lena的胸口听了个遍，最后请来上级；药费减半，但Lena被当成了讲课的病例。学生们走后，实习医生把听诊器在手里捂了很久，才敢再贴上来。

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000573:child; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1948.07** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.stitched` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Lena用鞋蜡把裂缝填上，又拿粗针和麻线把鞋底缝了一圈。针脚歪，鞋底一高一低，走路时右脚落地总比左脚重。第二天上工回来，脚后跟磨出一个水泡，可鞋没再渗水。
  _effects: skill_

- **1948.08** `r1-civ.requisition-cart::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/room-requisitioned` tags: war, requisition, property, army, shortage, vienna, urban, postwar
  Luise Kral要征用Lena家朝街的一间屋做办公室，给一张“使用许可”。Lena说屋里住着老人，Luise Kral说可以留一间后屋。搬东西那天，Mitzi Winter来帮忙，把墙上的十字架摘下来，说“这个先收好”。
  _effects: cash -3 (征收单上的马（其中3克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027; condition; flag +room_requisitioned; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; knowledge “the use permit leaves one back room to the household”_

- **1949.01** `r15-fam.school-registration::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/enrolls` tags: family, child, school, education, school_child, vienna, urban, postwar
  Lena带着Katharina Bauer去学校登记。孩子的名字被写在名册最后一行，墨水还没有干；回家的路上，Katharina Bauer一直捏着那张课程表，Lena则在算这个月还能剩下多少煤钱。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 39 -> 45; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 42 -> 20; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 55 -> 55; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 0 -> 0; state_parameter raising-child.schoolEnrolled false -> true; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> false; cash -1 (the first school books and a slate（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1949.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Katharina Bauer这周主动擦了黑板。Lena把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 28 -> 31; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013444_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Lena把Katharina Bauer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Katharina Bauer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Lena听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 28; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Lena把Anna Bauer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Anna Bauer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Lena听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 31 -> 39; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 42 -> 39; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.01** `r13-civ.donate-clothes::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/coat-donated` tags: social, charity, clothing, poverty, household, vienna, urban, postwar
  Lena把父亲留下的厚呢外套从箱底翻出来，掸掉樟脑味，叠好交给Katharina Winter。Katharina Winter摸了摸袖口，说这料子还能穿三年，就在收据上写了“男式冬衣一件”。回家时，箱子空出一角。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +donated_winter_coat; knowledge “the coat was logged as a man’s winter coat”_

- **1949.03** `r1-hc.cholera-cordon::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/cordon-hides-boarder` tags: health, epidemic, cholera, quarantine, neighbor, vienna, urban, postwar
  天没亮，巡警在院门口撒了一道石灰，说这条巷子只进不出。Lena家里还住着一个拉肚子的房客，Maria 2让把人抬到后院，别让防疫队看见；后院的柴堆后面，多了一张草垫。
  _effects: cash -8 (院子门口的石灰线（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +hid_sick_boarder_inside_cordon; role; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003607:child; knowledge “the epidemic squad counts people at the gate, not in the yar”_

- **1949.09** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  炉子封小了，屋里凉得快，Lena把大衣盖在被子上。安娜收碗时看见窗台上一排药瓶，没说什么，第二天多带了半块黑面包。楼下的管道工敲了一下午铁皮，咳嗽声混在里面听不真切。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143_

- **1949.10** `r1-civ.consulate-window::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/visa-postponed-photo` tags: migration, visa, passport, consulate, papers, vienna, urban, postwar
  Lena把护照和照片递进窗口，Otto Lechner 2翻到第二页，说照片上的耳朵被头发遮住了，要重照。Lena去对面的照相馆，回来时窗口已经关了。第二天再来，Otto Lechner 2说号排到明年三月；Lena把号码牌攥在手心，纸片被汗浸软。
  _effects: cash -3 (领事馆窗口的号码牌（其中3克朗记入债务）); mood -0.09000000000000008; flag +visa_postponed_photo; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-130; knowledge “the photograph must show both ears and the queue runs to nex”_

- **1950.01** `task-vienna-writer-researcher.abandon-project/project-sold` tags: knowledge, culture, writing, editor, library, publication, task, task:knowledge
  房租催到门口，Lena把已写的章节卖给一家小报，换了八克朗，够付一个月房租和两顿热饭。长篇锁进抽屉，钥匙搁在盐罐后面，Grete Winter 2问起时他只说“先不提了”，第二天照常去上工。
  _effects: cash 8 (小报买下章节的稿酬); mood -0.06000000000000005; reputation -0.01999999999999999; flag +novel_shelved; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-147; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-135_

- **1950.03** `vienna-church-community.saint-vincent-box/box-request-granted` tags: faith, social, family, home
  Lena在圣文生会门口登记，领到一张面包券和一小袋土豆。
  _effects: food; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1950.03** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Lena端着一只旧锅去敲Franz Moser的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Franz Moser让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Lena连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001378:child; food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.03** `r13-rel.remarriage::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::remarriage-postwar/two-widows-one-flat` tags: relationship, remarriage, marriage, courtship, household, vienna, urban, postwar
  两个人都失去了原来的伴侣，各自带着孩子。Lena把两家的床并到一间屋里，Anton Huber 3把两只箱子摞在墙角。第一顿饭，四个孩子抢一只锅，Anton Huber 3把最大的那块肉夹给Lena的孩子。
  _effects: cash -4 (第二次婚礼上的旧戒指（其中4克朗记入债务）); role; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; household; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1950.05** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Berta Bauer在午休时把本子摊在Lena的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Lena从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Berta Bauer当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱（其中0.3克朗记入债务）); relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004395:child; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

- **1951.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Anna 3。接生的人收六克朗，Anton Huber 3在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Lena起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中2克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:342_

- **1951.01** `r1-hc.asylum-admission::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::asylum-reform-observation/asylum-reform-released` tags: health, mental, asylum, consent, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  复核医生问了三个问题，又翻了翻Lena的工票，说没有明显危险，可以走。Anton Huber 3在门外接过她的外套，两人谁也没提刚才那间屋子，只说明天还得上班。
  _effects: cash -6 (两张证明和一支钢笔（其中6克朗记入债务）); condition; role; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; knowledge “the observation room releases patients who can show a work t”_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Lena被叫到学校，老师说Katharina Bauer这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Lena没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 31 -> 26; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 42 -> 47; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.01** `r13-work.practice-craft::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/joint-fits` tags: work, craft, practice, wood, skill, vienna, urban, postwar
  Lena把第四十个榫头敲进卯眼，声音是闷的，不晃。Mitzi Berg 3路过，用拇指按了按接缝，说这回不用胶也能站住。Lena把废掉的三十九个榫头扫进灶膛，火苗从锯末里窜起来，屋里全是松木的味。
  _effects: cash 2 (第四十个榫头); skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1951.02** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.quarrel` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  水龙头滴滴答答地漏，两人都伸手去扶桶沿。Lena看见自家未洗的床单还堆着，胸口发闷，把桶让了半掌宽。晾绳上她的衬衣角被风掀起，正挡着邻家的窗户。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003225:child; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

- **1951.03** `r14-soc.neighbor-wall::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/shared-the-mason` tags: neighbor_conflict, property, law, boundary, courtyard, vienna, urban, postwar
  Lena提议两家各出一半钱，请同一个泥瓦匠把墙砌直。Alois Hofer算了半天，最后把四克朗放在门槛上，说“料钱你出”。墙砌好那天，两家的孩子已经在墙根下玩同一副石子。泥瓦匠收工时要两家人一起签字，Alois Hofer先写了名字，Lena把日期补上。那袋石灰后来砌进了两家的门槛。
  _effects: cash -4 (半砖界墙（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood 0.06000000000000005; flag +shared_fence_cost; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

  Lena傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Friedrich Lechner在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Friedrich Lechner敲开Lena的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。

  Lena看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Franz Hofer 3追出来问怎么不领了，Lena说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Lena把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126_

- **1953.01** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.default` tags: home, market, food, social
  欠账拖了半个月。玛丽亚在菜摊前看见Lena路过，直接喊住要钱。Lena拿不出，玛丽亚把已经称好的土豆倒回筐里。Alois Winter提着篮子站在两步外，什么也没说。
  _effects: food; reputation -0.15; mood -0.09999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000447:child_

  Anton Huber 3在办事处说了Lena一整个下午的坏话，从欠债说到那场没人看见的火灾。旁边的人没有替Lena说一句话。几天后，Lena发现自己的名字从互助会的名单上被划掉了，Anton Huber 3只说“我也要养家”。
  _effects: reputation -0.08; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; cash -1 (互助会停止发放的补贴（其中1克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +reputation_damaged_by_betrayal_

- **1953.05** `r1-civ.mobilization-poster::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/reserved-for-munitions` tags: war, mobilization, army, homefront, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Lena被叫进兵营登记，Ferdinand Huber把她的职业写成“印刷工”，又在备注栏里加了“可留厂”。Lena问这算什么，Ferdinand Huber说军需订单比枪更需要人。走出兵营时，Lena看见一队新兵正在量身高，最矮的那个还没长胡子。
  _effects: flag +reserved_for_munitions_work; role; knowledge “a skilled printer can be reserved for munitions orders”; employment Munitionsarbeiter @ org-winter-print; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-139_

  解放一周年，广场上立了一块临时木牌，写着“感谢”。Lena发现自己的名字被刻在“合作者”那一栏，因为战时在占领军厨房干过活。Alois Hofer替Lena作证，说Lena往汤里多兑了水，救过不少人；Otto 2听完，让人把木牌上的字刨掉。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; reputation 0.07999999999999999; flag +cleared_of_collaboration_charge; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001893:child; knowledge “my name was carved under collaborators”_

  Otto Lechner 2坐在窗边，要求把下巴画窄一寸，把勋章画大两指。Lena照做了，颜料未干就拿到尾款十八克朗。Otto Lechner 2把画挂进会客厅，逢人便说是“老朋友的手笔”。Maria Bauer在厨房听见，低声问那幅画里到底有没有一个人，Lena说画的是Otto Lechner 2愿意付钱的那张脸。
  _effects: cash 18 (赞助人袖口的金线); reputation 0.08; flag +spec14_patron_kept; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-130_

  Luise Kral把申请推回来，说“推料工不在短缺工种名单上”，要雇主先证明招不到本地人。Lena回到住处，房东已经把那间小屋租给了别人。行李放在楼梯口，煤油炉还在箱子里。Luise Kral说的下一句话是“下一位”。
  _effects: cash -2 (劳工证上的工种栏); mood -0.12; condition; flag +permit_refused_shortage_list; knowledge “the shortage list excludes factory helpers”; unemployment (work permit refused: job not on the shortage list)_

- **1954.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Anna Stein在门口替Lena捏着帽子，听见里面一声闷哼。弗朗茨把牙扔进搪瓷盘，说瞧这牙根都黑了。收钱时他多要了二十赫勒，算明矾水的钱，说下次别再拖成这样。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1955.01** `r14-soc.neighbor-rescue::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/carried-through-smoke` tags: neighbor_rescue, saved_a_life, fire, neighbor_conflict, courage, vienna, urban, postwar
  Alois Hofer家的厨房先冒烟，火从煤箱后面蹿上来。Lena用湿毛巾捂住口鼻，把倒在门边的Alois Hofer拖过两级台阶。救护车到时，Lena的眉毛已经燎掉一半，怀里还抱着对方那只装证件的铁盒。消防员把烧黑的锅拎出来时，Lena才觉得手掌开始疼。担架抬走时，Lena还问了一句“铁盒带出来没有”。
  _effects: health -0.1 (三楼冒烟的厨房); condition; flag +saved_neighbor_from_fire; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014656_

  Ferdinand 2用拇指甲刮着一件领子的边线，说这儿跳了针。Lena把煤油灯往前推了推，让他凑近看，说线是细了点，活没少做。Helene Moser倚在门框上没走，屋里一时只有尺子碰桌面的响。

  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

  Lena在Mitzi Winter的账本上按了手印，借十克朗，周息五分。Mitzi Winter把钱数在柜台上，又抽回一枚，说那是“跑腿费”。Anna Lechner 2在门口看着，等Mitzi Winter进屋才低声说，这条街上没人还得清他的第二笔。

  Lena的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1955.07** `statistical_recovery`
  Lena从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1955.12** `major-family.separation-widowhood/separation-widowhood.widowed-tin-box` tags: family, separation, widowhood, housing, major, lifecycle, death, injury
  Anton Huber 3咳了半年，最后一个月连楼梯都上不去。Lena把床让给对方，自己睡在厨房的长凳上。Anton Huber 3死在清晨，Lena去市政办事处登记，又去Anton Huber 3上工的地方结清工钱；葬礼从简，神父念完经，Lena把Anton Huber 3的工票和剃刀收进一只铁盒。
  _effects: death (pulmonary tuberculosis); health -0.06 (nursing and grief before the funeral); role; mood -0.050000000000000044; cash 8 (final wages collected from the partner's workplace); cash -12 (funeral, mass and black clothes)_

- **1955.12** `r1-work.market-stall-allotment::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/bought-the-middle-place` tags: commerce, market, stall, permit, rivalry, vienna, urban, postwar
  Lena把五克朗折成小方块，压在Ferdinand Huber的登记簿下面。Ferdinand Huber没有抬头，只把粉笔往中间挪了半尺。第二天Lena的摊子到了棚中央，Elisabeth Moser的苹果墙被挤到排水沟边，有几只果子滚进了泥里。旁边卖菜的妇人把秤往远处挪了挪，Elisabeth Moser弯腰捡苹果时低声骂了一句；Ferdinand Huber装作没听见，只把粉笔头碾碎在鞋底。
  _effects: cash -5 (棚下的第三个摊位); reputation -0.04000000000000001; flag +bribed_market_master_

- **1956.01** `r14-soc.favor-obligation::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/not-a-contract` tags: favor_obligation, neighbor_rescue, debt, obligation, neighbor_conflict, vienna, urban, postwar
  Lena把门开着一半，说救人的时候没想过要收账。Alois Hofer的脸慢慢硬起来，说“行，算我白救”。门关上后，走廊里很久没有脚步声。第二天，两家的垃圾各自堆在各自的门边。那年冬天，两家都没有再借过煤；走廊里的灯坏了，也没有人换。开春后，那盏灯还是坏的。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +refused_favor_claim; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017281_

- **1956.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library/p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library.pay-deposit` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:knowledge
  Lena从内袋里数出两克朗压在柜台上，Franz Lechner 5翻开借书卡，用铅笔把上次迟还的四天圈出来，还是把小说递了过来。Lena把书塞进外套里侧，出门就翻到第一页，边走边读，差点撞上推手推车的菜贩；两克朗换来的不只是一本小说，还有一整个下午的安静。
